# WSMM Family Board E8027FAA pogo pin mapping

The family board of the Wave Scale Millimeter (WSMM) solution maps custom configured DUT Scale interface resources to the pogo blocks of the WSMM Card Cage DUT interface. The mapping of the individual pogo pins is shown in the following table.

The tables cover the mapping of the pogo pins for the WSMM Family Board E8027FAA (E8027-66402, Rev. 1).

For the mapping of the pogo blocks, refer to [WSMM Family Board E8027FAA pogo block mapping](<02.03.06.07.02.02.01_WSMM_Family_Board_E8027FAA_pogo_block_mapping.md>).

## Important restrictions

 The following restrictions must be respected when designing a DUT board for the WSMM solution:

- Digital channels

  - 11615 (WSMM block.pin = 6418.A16)
  - 11616 (WSMM block.pin = 6418.B17)
are normally routed to the DUT interface but can be configured to control the WSMM Card Cage switches in the old way. For this purpose, switches are inserted into the signal path on the family board and the impedance is therefore slightly different from that of the other channels.
- Digital channels

  - 11613 (WSMM block.pin = 6418.A14)
  - 11614 (WSMM block.pin = 6418.D15)
have longer routes on the family board in order to match pins 11615 and 11616 as differential pairs.
- UP5V supply current capability on utility blocks

  - UTIL 4 (WSMM block.pin = 7225.A05 and 7225.B04)
  - UTIL 5 (WSMM block.pin = 4129.A05 and 4129.B04)
  - UTIL 8 (WSMM block.pin = 7221.A05 and 7221.B04)
is restricted depending on the installed RMMs.

## Pogo pin mapping

The following table shows the mapping from pogo pin signals of the DUT Scale interface (DS) to the [WSMM Card Cage DUT Interface](<02.03.06.07.02.01_WSMM_Card_Cage_DUT_Interface.md>) (WSMM).

 Some notes on the table:

- DS usage "DGND plane": Ground of all digital pins.
- DS usage "UGND plane": Return path for utility voltages UP5V, XPP5V, XPP3V3, XPM5V and all utility lines.
- DS block "###" (###): Additional signals of high density pogo blocks. The first three digits define the physical pogo block location. The next three digits in brackets define a [virtual pogo block](<02.03.03.01_DUT_interface_layout.md>) for the additional signals.
- DS block "DPS 15" and "DPS 34": Common ground of the 2nd DPS128 that uses MS-DPS style pogo blocks.
- WSMM block: The physical pogo block of the WSMM Card Cage DUT interface.
- WSMM logical pin: The pin designation that is used by SmarTest. The logical pin is basically build from the WSMM pogo block and the signal 01 鈥?16. For pogo blocks with more than 16 signals, an offset of 400 is added to the number of the physical pogo block to generate a [virtual pogo block.](<02.03.06.07.02.01_WSMM_Card_Cage_DUT_Interface.md#_348744__s_Virtual_pogo_blocks>)
- WSMM type: The type of the WSMM Card Cage pogo block:

  - Digital: E8027CXP coaxial pogo block for digital/mixed signal
  - DPS: E8027DUP pogo block for DPS/Utility. Maximum 1 A per pogo pin.
For more information, refer to the "Wave Scale Millimeter products summary" in Wave Scale Millimeter solution.
- Pogo blocks 305/5403 can be configured for the WSMX card or for the PS9G card. Maximum one WSMX card is supported by the family board if all four PS9G pogo blocks are to be routed to the WSMX DUT interface. For the second (optional) WSMX card, only one of the two WSMX‑HD pogo blocks can be routed to the WSMX DUT interface.
